        var url = window.location;
        if (!url.href.includes("ezVendWelcome")) {
            const allLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.nav .nav-item a');
            let currentLink = [...allLinks].find(e => {
                return e.href === url.href.replaceAll('#', '');
            });
            if (!currentLink) {

            if (currentLink) {

        // the customized scrollbar is added when the drop down is opened or 
        // there is an input
        function customizeDropdownScrollbar()
        {
            setTimeout(function ()
            {
                // count the number of elements which can be used for selection in the drop down
                var count = $('#select2-CatalogArea-results').find("li").length;
                var count1 = $('#select2-Vndr-results').find("li").length;

                // check if a previous instance of the customized scrollbar already exists
                if (typeof instance !== 'undefined' && (count > 1 || count1 > 1))
                {
                    // destrop the previous instance
                    instance.destroy();
                }

                // assign a scrollbar to a different element according to the number 
                // of available elements in the drop down
                // trick to fix the customization of the scrollbar when there is one 
                // or zero element to select
                if (count > 1 || count1 > 1)
                {
                    // create and store the instance reference for future destruction
                    var overlayScrollbarsTheme = "os-theme-dark";
                    instance = $('#select2-CatalogArea-results, #select2-Vndr-results').overlayScrollbars(
                            {
                                className: overlayScrollbarsTheme,
                                resize: "none",
                                scrollbars:
                                        {
                                            visibility: "auto",
                                            autoHide: "move",
                                            autoHideDelay: 800,
                                            dragScrolling: true,
                                            clickScrolling: false,
                                            touchSupport: true,
                                            snapHandle: false
                                        },
                            }).overlayScrollbars();
                } else
                {
                    // assign the scrollbar to an higher level in the hierarchy of the drop down
                    $('.select2-dropdown select2-dropdown--below').overlayScrollbars(
                            {
                                className: overlayScrollbarsTheme,
                                resize: "none",
                                scrollbars:
                                        {
                                            visibility: "visible",
                                            autoHide: "never",
                                        },
                            }).overlayScrollbars();
                }
            }, 0);
        }

        /*********************** Avatar Upload Script ******************************/

        var readURL = function (input) {
            if (input.files && input.files[0]) {
                var reader = new FileReader();

                reader.onload = function (e) {
                    $('.profile-pic').attr('src', e.target.result);
                    if (localStorage)
                        localStorage.setItem("profilePic", userId + "$$" + e.target.result);
                }

                reader.readAsDataURL(input.files[0]);
            }
        }

        $(".file-upload").on('change', function () {
            readURL(this);
        });

        $(".upload-button").on('click', function () {
            $(".file-upload").click();
        });

        if (localStorage.getItem("profilePic")) {

            let userData = localStorDate.split('$$')[1];

        });
        /********************* End *************************************/

    });

    function callFun(clickedOn)
    {
        if (clickedOn === 'H')
        {

        } else if (clickedOn === 'L')

    var prevSelectedVal;

    });
    $('#CatalogArea').on("select2:select", function ()
    {
        var selElement = document.getElementById('CatalogArea');
        var selcatareaTxt = selElement.options[selElement.selectedIndex].innerHTML;
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Purchase Area Change.',
            text: "Do you want to continue with the selected Purchase Area?",
            icon: 'warning',
            width: 600,
            confirmButtonColor: '#5cb85c',
            cancelButtonColor: '#a91f33',
            showConfirmButton: true,
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: 'Yes',
            cancelButtonText: 'No',
            allowOutsideClick: false,
            input: 'checkbox',
            inputPlaceholder: 'Make ' + selcatareaTxt + ' your default  selection?'
//            reverseButtons: true
        }).then((result) => {
            if (result.isConfirmed) {

                var selcatarea = document.headerForm.CatalogArea.value;
                if (result.value)
                {
                    $.ajax({
                        url: "ezSetDefaultSysKey.jsp",
                        data: {
                            'selcatarea': selcatarea
                        },
                        type: "POST"
                    });
                }
                document.headerForm.callPreWelcomePage.value = "Y";
                document.headerForm.changePurArea.value = "Y";
                document.headerForm.action = "../Misc/ezVendWelcome.jsp?CatalogArea=" + selcatarea;
                document.headerForm.submit();

            } else
            {
                $('#CatalogArea').val(prevSelectedVal).trigger('change');
                return false;
            }
        });
    });
    function changeVendor(mFlag)
    {
        document.headerForm.callPreWelcomePage.value = "S";
        if (mFlag == 'Y')
        {

            document.headerForm.target = "banner"
            document.headerForm.action = "../Misc/ezVendWelcome.jsp";
            document.headerForm.submit();
        } else
        {
            Swal.fire({
                title: 'Vendor Change',
                text: "Do you want to continue with the selected vendor?",
                icon: 'warning',
                width: 600,
                confirmButtonColor: '#5cb85c',
                cancelButtonColor: '#a91f33',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonText: 'Yes',
                cancelButtonText: 'No',
//                reverseButtons: true
            }).then((result) => {
                if (result.isConfirmed) {
                    var vendorDetails = document.headerForm.Vndr.options[document.headerForm.Vndr.options.selectedIndex].value
                    var vendorData = vendorDetails.split("#")
                    document.headerForm.VENDOR_CODE.value = vendorData[0]
                    document.headerForm.VENDOR_NAME.value = vendorData[1]
                    //document.headerForm.target="banner"
                    document.headerForm.action = "../Misc/ezVendWelcome.jsp";
                    document.headerForm.submit();

                } else
                    document.headerForm.Vndr.options[document.headerForm.selVNIndex.value].selected = true;
            });

        }
    }
    function modelMatcher(params, data) {
        data.parentText = data.parentText || "";

        // Always return the object if there is nothing to compare
        if ($.trim(params.term) === '') {
            return data;
        }

        // Do a recursive check for options with children
        if (data.children && data.children.length > 0) {
            // Clone the data object if there are children
            // This is required as we modify the object to remove any non-matches
            var match = $.extend(true, {}, data);

            // Check each child of the option
            for (var c = data.children.length - 1; c >= 0; c--) {
                var child = data.children[c];
                child.parentText += data.parentText + " " + data.text;

                var matches = modelMatcher(params, child);

                // If there wasn't a match, remove the object in the array
                if (matches == null) {
                    match.children.splice(c, 1);
                }
            }

            // If any children matched, return the new object
            if (match.children.length > 0) {
                return match;
            }

            // If there were no matching children, check just the plain object
            return modelMatcher(params, match);
        }

        // If the typed-in term matches the text of this term, or the text from any
        // parent term, then it's a match.
        var original = (data.parentText + ' ' + data.text).toUpperCase();
        var term = params.term.toUpperCase();


        // Check if the text contains the term
        if (original.indexOf(term) > -1) {
            return data;
        }

        // If it doesn't contain the term, don't return anything
        return null;
    }

</script>

<script type="text/javascript" class="init">
    var table;

            "autoWidth": false,

	            { visible: false, targets: [6, 13, 14, 15] }
	        
	    ],

            buttons: [
                {
                    extend: 'excelHtml5',
                    text: 'Download To Excel',
                    title: null,
                    filename: 'Booking List',
                    className: "buttons-excel",
                    exportOptions: {                        
                        
                        columns: [0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12]
                        
                        
                    }
                }
            ]
        });
        table.buttons().container().appendTo('.downExcel');

        $('.buttons-excel').on('click', function () {

            let that = this;
            this.disabled = true;
            let btnText = this.innerHTML;
            this.innerHTML = '<i class="fa fa-download faa-falling animated" style="padding-right:5px"></i>' + btnText;

                    .visible(false);

        if (status !== 'B' && status !== 'F' && status !== 'R') {
            //$('.optionalTd').hide();
            table.column('.optionalTd').visible(false);

	 $('#getBookingsBtn').click(function () {
	     var fromDate = $('#fromDate').val();
	     var toDate = $('#toDate').val();
	 
	     if (fromDate && toDate) {
	         // Convert date strings to Date objects for comparison
	         var fromDateObj = new Date(fromDate);
	         var toDateObj = new Date(toDate);
	 
	         // Check if To Date is greater than From Date
	         if (toDateObj < fromDateObj) {
	             alert('To Date must be greater than From Date');
	         } else {
	             var statusFromParam = "C";
	             var currentUrl = window.location.href.split('?')[0];
	             var newUrl = currentUrl + '?status=' + encodeURIComponent(statusFromParam) +
	                 '&fromDate=' + fromDate +
	                 '&toDate=' + toDate;
	 
	             window.location.href = newUrl;
	         }
	     } else {
	         alert('Please select both From Date and To Date');
	     }
	 });

    $(document).on('click', '.sendMessage', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        let email = $('#recipients').val();
        if (email)
            email = email.replaceAll(';', ',');
        if (email.lastIndexOf(',') > 0)
            email = email.substring(0, email.lastIndexOf(','))
            
        let ccEmail = $('#ccusers').val();
        if (ccEmail)
            ccEmail = ccEmail.replaceAll(';', ',');
      //  if (ccEmail.lastIndexOf(',') > 0)
      //      ccEmail = ccEmail.substring(0, ccEmail.lastIndexOf(','))             
            
        let emailText = $('#message-text').val();
        let bookingId = $('#bookingId').val();
        //alert("bookingId...."+bookingId);
        let agentName = $('#agent').val();

        if (email) {

            $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: "ezSendShipEmail.jsp", 
                data: {
                    email: email,
                    ccEmail :ccEmail,
                    text: emailText,
                    bookingId: bookingId,
                    agent: agentName,
                    sendMail: 'Y'
                },
                beforeSend: function (e) {

                },
                success: function (response) {
                    console.log(response);
                    if (response.trim() === "Y") {
                        $('#mailForm').trigger('reset');
                        $("#exampleModal").modal('toggle');
                        toastr.success('An Mail has been sent to FreightForwarder for the selected booking id.</br>');
                        location.reload();
                    } else
                    {
                        toastr.error('Unable to send email. Please try again11.');
                    }

                },
                error: function () {
                    $("#exampleModal").modal('toggle');
                    toastr.error('Unable to send email. Please try again.');

                }

            });
        }

    });
    $('#exampleModal').on('show.bs.modal', function (event) {

        var button = $(event.relatedTarget) // Button that triggered the modal
        var bookingObj = button.closest('tr').find('input').val();
        var bookingId = bookingObj.split("¥¥")[0] ? bookingObj.split("¥¥")[0] : "";
        var countryVal = bookingObj.split("¥¥")[1] ? bookingObj.split("¥¥")[1] : "";
        var portVal = bookingObj.split("¥¥")[2] ? bookingObj.split("¥¥")[2] : "";
        var agentVal = bookingObj.split("¥¥")[3] ? bookingObj.split("¥¥")[3] : "";
        //alert("bookingObj...."+bookingObj);
        $.ajax({
            type: 'POST',
            url: "../../../Includes/JSPs/Shipment/iGetFreightForwaders.jsp",
            beforeSend: function (e) {
                $(".pre-loader").toggle();
            },
            success: function (response) {
                //console.log(response);
                try
                {
                    response = response.trim();
                    var json = $.parseJSON(response);

                    for (var i = 0; i < json.length; ++i)
                    {
                        if (country.findIndex(obj => obj.id === json[i][0]) === -1)
                            country.push({'id': json[i][0], 'text': json[i][0]});
                        var cuntPort = json[i][0] + "¥¥" + json[i][1];
                        if (port.indexOf(cuntPort) === -1)
                            port.push(cuntPort);
                        var portAgent = json[i][1] + "¥¥" + json[i][2];
                        if (agent.indexOf(portAgent) === -1)
                            agent.push(portAgent);
                        var portAgentEmailStr = json[i][1] + "¥¥" + json[i][2] + "¥¥" + json[i][3];
                        if (emails.indexOf(portAgentEmailStr) === -1)
                            emails.push(portAgentEmailStr);
                    }
                    $("#originCountry").select2({data: country});
                    if (countryVal && country.findIndex(obj => obj.id == countryVal) !== -1) {
                        $("#originCountry").val(countryVal).trigger('change');
                        $("#originCountry").prop("disabled", true);
                        $('b[role="presentation"]').hide();
                    }
                    var countrySel = $("#originCountry").val();
                    if (countrySel)
                    {
                        var portArr = [];
                        port.forEach(function (index) {
                            //console.log(index);
                            var cuntPort = index;
                            if (cuntPort.split("¥¥")[0] === countrySel)
                            {
                                portArr.push({'id': cuntPort.split("¥¥")[1], 'text': cuntPort.split("¥¥")[1]});
                            }
                        });
                        $("#originPort").select2({data: portArr});
                        if (portVal && portArr.findIndex(obj => obj.id === portVal) !== -1)
                        {
                            $("#originPort").val(portVal).trigger('change');
                            $("#originPort").prop("disabled", true);
                            $('b[role="presentation"]').hide();
                        }
                    }
                    var portSel = $("#originPort").val();
                    if (portSel)
                    {
                        var agentArr = [];
                        agent.forEach(function (index) {
                            //console.log(index);
                            var portAgent = index;
                            if (portAgent.split("¥¥")[0] === portSel)
                            {
                                agentArr.push({'id': portAgent.split("¥¥")[1], 'text': portAgent.split("¥¥")[1]});
                            }
                        });
                        $("#agent").select2({data: agentArr});
                        if (agentVal && agentArr.findIndex(obj => obj.id === agentVal) !== -1)
                        {
                            $("#agent").val(agentVal).trigger('change');
                            $("#agent").prop("disabled", true);
                            $('b[role="presentation"]').hide();
                        }
                    }

                    $(".pre-loader").toggle();
                } catch (err)
                {
                    $(".pre-loader").toggle();
                }

            },
            error: function () {
                $(".pre-loader").toggle();
            }

        });
        var modal = $(this)
        modal.find('.modal-title').text('Share Booking Info to the Freight Forwarder')
        //modal.find('.modal-body input').val(recipient);
        modal.find('.modal-body #bookingId').val(bookingId);

        $('#message-text').summernote({
            placeholder: 'Your Comments..',
            tabsize: 2,
            height: 100
        });

    });
    $('#exampleModal').on('change', '#originCountry', function () {
        $("#originPort").val('').trigger('change');
        $("#originPort").empty();
        var countrySel = $("#originCountry").val();
        if (countrySel)
        {
            var portArr = [];
            portArr.push({'id': '', 'text': ''});
            port.forEach(function (index) {
                var cuntPort = index;
                if (cuntPort.split("¥¥")[0] === countrySel)
                {
                    portArr.push({'id': cuntPort.split("¥¥")[1], 'text': cuntPort.split("¥¥")[1]});
                }
            });
            $("#originPort").select2({data: portArr});
        }

    });
    $('#exampleModal').on('change', '#originPort', function () {
        $("#agent").val('').trigger('change');
        $("#agent").empty();
        var portSel = $("#originPort").val();
        if (portSel)
        {
            var agentArr = [];
            agentArr.push({'id': '', 'text': ''});
            agent.forEach(function (index) {
                //console.log(index);
                var portAgent = index;
                if (portAgent.split("¥¥")[0] === portSel)
                {
                    agentArr.push({'id': portAgent.split("¥¥")[1], 'text': portAgent.split("¥¥")[1]});
                }
            });
            $("#agent").select2({data: agentArr});
        }
    });

    $('#exampleModal').on('change', '#agent', function () {
        $("#recipients").val('');
        var agentSel = $("#agent").val();
        var portSel = $("#originPort").val();
        if (agentSel && portSel)
        {
            var emailStr = [];
            emails.forEach(function (index) {
                //console.log(index);
                var emailObj = index;
                if (emailObj.split("¥¥")[0] === portSel && emailObj.split("¥¥")[1] === agentSel)
                {
                    emailStr += emailObj.split("¥¥")[2] + "; ";
                }
            });
            $("#recipients").val(emailStr);
        } else
        {
            $("#recipients").val("");
        }
    });
    $('#exampleModal').on('hidden.bs.modal', function (event) {

        $('#message-text').summernote('destroy');
        $("#exampleModal").load(location.href + " #exampleModal>*", "");

    });

    function funDetails(bookingNo, status, selPOs, docUsrGroup, poType, originPort)
    {
        var uType='FF';
        document.myForm.status.value = status
        if(uType=="LF" && "VENDGRP"==docUsrGroup)
        {
        	if( (poType=="Z2" || poType=="Z3" || poType=="Z7" || poType=="Z8") && originPort!="")
        		document.myForm.action = "ezViewBookingDetails.jsp?bookingId=" + bookingNo;
        	else if(status=="B")
        		document.myForm.action = "ezAddShipmentDetails.jsp?bookingId=" + bookingNo+"&ponum="+selPOs;
        	else
        		document.myForm.action = "ezViewBookingDetails.jsp?bookingId=" + bookingNo;
        }
        else
        {
        	var chkBoxStatus ='S';
        	//document.myForm.action = "ezViewBookingDetails.jsp?bookingId=" + bookingNo; 
        	document.myForm.action = "ezViewBookingDetails.jsp?bookingId=" + bookingNo + "&chkBoxStatus=" + chkBoxStatus;
        }
        document.myForm.submit();
    }

    function openModal(modalHeader, modalContent)
    {

        $('.modal-header').html(modalHeader);
        $('#rejectReason').html(modalContent);
        $('#woModal').modal({show: true});
    }

    function closeIframe()
    {
        $('#woModal').modal('hide');
    }
    var req;
    var bkngID;
    function funDel()
    {
    	bkngID = document.myForm.bkngID.value;
    	//alert("bkngID>>"+bkngID);
    	if(bkngID === "") 
    	{
		toastr.error('Select booking id for deletion');
    	}
    	else
    	{
		try{
			req = new ActiveXObject("Msxml2.XMLHTTP");
		}
		catch(e)
		{
		try{
			req = new ActiveXObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP");
		}
		catch(oc)
		{
			req = null;
		}
		}  
		if(!req&&typeof XMLHttpRequest!="undefined"){
			req = new XMLHttpRequest();
		}
		var url="ezDelBooking.jsp?bkngID="+bkngID;
		if(req!=null){
			req.onreadystatechange = Process;
			req.open("GET", url, true);
			req.send(null);
		}	
    	}
    } 
function Process()
{
	if (req.readyState == 4)
	{
		var resText = req.responseText;	 	
		resText = resText.trim();
			
		if(resText=="Y")
		{
                        toastr.success('Booking ID has been deleted successfully');
                        location.reload();
                }else{
			toastr.error('Error in deleting booking id. Please try again.');    
		}
			
	}
}


var bookArr = new Array();
function funChecked(tis)
{
	//alert(tis.checked+">>"+tis.value);
	if(tis.checked)
	{
		bookArr.push(tis.value);
	}
	else
	{
		var pos=bookArr.indexOf(tis.value);
		bookArr.splice(pos, 1);//we will remove only 1 item from array everytime
	}
}

function funMultiple()
{
	if(bookArr.length==0)
	{
		//alert("Please select checkbox to view Booking Details");
		toastr.error("Please select checkbox to view Booking Details");
		return;
	}
	var chkBoxStatus
	if (bookArr.length === 1) {
	    chkBoxStatus = 'S';
	   
	    
	} else if (bookArr.length > 1) {
	    chkBoxStatus = 'M';
	    
	    
        }
	var poTypeArr = new Array();
	var originPortArr = new Array();
	var sLocationArr = new Array();
	for (var i = 0; i < bookArr.length; ++i){
	        var poType = eval("document.myForm.poType"+bookArr[i]).value;
	        var originPort = eval("document.myForm.originPort"+bookArr[i]).value;
	        var sLocation = eval("document.myForm.sLocation"+bookArr[i]).value;
	    
	        
		poTypeArr.push(poType);
		if (!originPortArr.includes(originPort))
			originPortArr.push(originPort);
			console.log("originPort:::::::"+originPort);
		if (!sLocationArr.includes(sLocation))
			sLocationArr.push(sLocation);
		console.log("sLocation:::::::"+sLocation);
	} 
	if (poTypeArr.includes("Z1") || poTypeArr.includes("Z4")) {
	    if (poTypeArr.includes("Z5") || poTypeArr.includes("Z6")) {
	        //alert("Consolidation is not allowed for the following PO types");
	        toastr.error("Consolidation is not allowed for the selected Bookings");
	        return;
	    }
	}
	
	if (poTypeArr.includes("Z5") || poTypeArr.includes("Z6")) {
	    if (poTypeArr.includes("Z1") || poTypeArr.includes("Z4")) {
	        //alert("Consolidation is not allowed for the following PO types");
	        toastr.error("Consolidation is not allowed for the selected Bookings");
	        return;
	    }
	}
	
        if(originPortArr.length>1){
        	 //alert("consolidated must have origin of port in common");
        	 toastr.error("Port of Origin should be same for Consolidation");
        	 return;
        }
        if(sLocationArr.length>1){
		 
		 toastr.error("Storage Location should be same for Consolidation");
		 return;
        }
        
	document.myForm.status.value = 'B';
	var allBookgs=bookArr.join();
	//alert("poTypeArr>>"+poTypeArr);
	//document.myForm.action = "ezViewBookingDetails.jsp?bookingId=" + allBookgs;
	document.myForm.action = "ezViewBookingDetails.jsp?bookingId=" + allBookgs + "&chkBoxStatus=" + chkBoxStatus;

	document.myForm.submit();
}

</script>
